Match currency symbol followed by numeric part with commas\n    match = re.match(r'^([^\\d]*)([\\d,]+)$', value)\n    if match:\n        currency = match.group(1)\n        number = match.group(2).replace(',', '')\n        return currency + number\n    return value\n\ndef parse_date_range(date_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a date range string like '2026-08 ~ 2026-10' or '2027-01'.\n    Returns (start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern for range: YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM\n    range_match = re.search(r'(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})', date_str)\n    if range_match:\n        start_year = int(range_match.group(1))\n        start_month = int(range_match.group(2))\n        end_year = int(range_match.group(3))\n        end_month = int(range_match.group(4))\n        return start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month\n\n    # Pattern for single month: YYYY-MM\n    single_match = re.search(r'(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})', date_str)\n    if single_match:\n        year = int(single_match.group(1))\n        month = int(single_match.group(2))\n        return year, month, year, month\n\n    return None\n\ndef calculate_months(start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate inclusive month count between two dates.\n    Formula: (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month + 1)\n    \"\"\"\n    return (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month + 1)\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replace all {{placeholder}} patterns in text with values from data.\n    Returns (new_text, modified_flag).\n    \"\"\"\n    if text is None:\n        return text, False\n\n    modified = False\n    pattern = r'\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}'\n\n    def replace_match(match):\n        nonlocal modified\n        key = match.group(1)\n        if key in data:\n            modified = True\n            value = data[key]\n            # Normalize budget if this is the budget field\n            if key == '예산':\n                value = normalize_budget(value)\n            return value\n        return match.group(0)\n\n    new_text = re.sub(pattern, replace_match, text)\n    return new_text, modified\n\ndef append_month_spans(text, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    For phase lines (단계1, 단계2, 단계3), calculate and append month span.\n    Returns (new_text, modified_flag).\n    \"\"\"\n    if text is None:\n        return text, False\n\n    # Map phase prefixes to their data keys\n    phase_prefixes = [\n        ('1단계: ', '단계1'),\n        ('2단계: ', '단계2'),\n        ('3단계: ', '단계3'),\n    ]\n\n    for prefix, key in phase_prefixes:\n        if text.startswith(prefix):\n            # Get the phase value from data\n            phase_value = data.get(key, '')\n            # Parse the date range from the phase value\n            date_range = parse_date_range(phase_value)\n            if date_range:\n                start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month = date_range\n                months = calculate_months(start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month)\n                # Append the month span\n                new_text = text + f' ({months}개월)'\n                return new_text, True\n            break\n\n    return text, False\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process an XML file: replace placeholders, append month spans, remove layout cache.\n    Returns the modified XML as bytes.\n    \"\"\"\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # Namespace\n    ns = {'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'}\n\n    # Track modified paragraphs\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    # Process all elements\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        # Process text content\n        if elem.text:\n            new_text, text_modified = replace_placeholders_in_text(elem.text, data)\n            if text_modified:\n                elem.text = new_text\n                # Mark parent paragraph as modified\n                parent = elem\n                while parent is not None:\n                    if parent.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}p':\n                        modified_paras.add(parent)\n                        break\n                    parent = parent.find('..')\n\n            # For phase lines, also append month spans\n            new_text, month_modified = append_month_spans(elem.text, data)\n            if month_modified:\n                elem.text = new_text\n                # Mark parent paragraph as modified\n                parent = elem\n                while parent is not None:\n                    if parent.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}p':\n                        modified_paras.add(parent)\n                        break\n                    parent = parent.find('..')\n\n        # Process tail content\n        if elem.tail:\n            new_tail, tail_modified = replace_placeholders_in_text(elem.tail, data)\n            if tail_modified:\n                elem.tail = new_tail\n\n    # Remove layout-cache elements from modified paragraphs\n    for para in modified_paras:\n        # Find and remove all hp:lineSegArray children\n        for child in list(para):\n            if child.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}lineSegArray':\n                para.remove(child)\n            elif child.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}lineSeg':\n                para.remove(child)\n\n    # Convert to bytes with proper XML declaration\n    return ET.tostring(root, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    # Load JSON data\n    data = load_json_data(JSON_PATH)\n    print(f\"Loaded data: {data}\")\n\n    # Verify budget normalization\n    print(f\"Budget normalized: {normalize_budget(data['예산'])}\")\n\n    # Process XML files\n    section0_path = WORK_DIR / 'Contents' / 'section0.xml'\n    section1_path = WORK_DIR / 'Contents' / 'section1.xml'\n\n    section0_xml = process_xml_file(section0_path, data)\n    section1_xml = process_xml_file(section1_path, data)\n\n    # Verify no placeholders remain\n    section0_str = section0_xml.decode('utf-8')\n    section1_str = section1_xml.decode('utf-8')\n\n    if '{{' in section0_str or '{{' in section1_str:\n        print(\"ERROR: Placeholders still remain in the output!\")\n        # Find and print remaining placeholders\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', section0_str):\n            print(f\"  Remaining in section0: {m.group()}\")\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', section1_str):\n            print(f\"  Remaining in section1: {m.group()}\")\n        raise ValueError(\"Unreplaced placeholders found!\")\n\n    print(\"All placeholders replaced successfully.\")\n\n    # Pattern for range: YYYY-MM ~ YYYY-MM\n    range_match = re.search(r'(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})', date_str)\n    if range_match:\n        start_year = int(range_match.group(1))\n        start_month = int(range_match.group(2))\n        end_year = int(range_match.group(3))\n        end_month = int(range_match.group(4))\n        return start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month\n\n    # Pattern for single month: YYYY-MM\n    single_match = re.search(r'(\\d{4})-(\\d{2})', date_str)\n    if single_match:\n        year = int(single_match.group(1))\n        month = int(single_match.group(2))\n        return year, month, year, month\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef calculate_months(start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate inclusive month count between two dates.\n    Formula: (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month + 1)\n    \"\"\"\n    return (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month + 1)\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replace all {{placeholder}} patterns in text with values from data.\n    Returns (new_text, modified_flag).\n    \"\"\"\n    if text is None:\n        return text, False\n\n    modified = False\n    pattern = r'\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}'\n\n    def replace_match(match):\n        nonlocal modified\n        key = match.group(1)\n        if key in data:\n            modified = True\n            value = data[key]\n            # Map phase prefixes to their data keys\n    phase_prefixes = [\n        ('1단계: ', '단계1'),\n        ('2단계: ', '단계2'),\n        ('3단계: ', '단계3'),\n    ]\n\n    for prefix, key in phase_prefixes:\n        if text.startswith(prefix):\n            phase_value = data.get(key, '')\n            date_range = parse_date_range(phase_value)\n            if date_range:\n                start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month = date_range\n                months = calculate_months(start_year, start_month, end_year, end_month)\n                new_text = text + f' ({months}개월)'\n                return new_text, True\n            break\n\n    return text, False\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process an XML file: replace placeholders, append month spans, remove layout cache.\n    Returns the modified XML as bytes.\n    \"\"\"\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # For phase lines, append month spans\n                new_text, month_modified = append_month_spans(text_elem.text, data)\n                if month_modified:\n                    text_elem.text = new_text\n                    para_modified = True\n\n        if para_modified:\n            modified_para_ids.add(para_id)\n\n    # Remove layout-cache elements from modified paragraphs\n    for para in root.iter(f'{HP_TAG}p'):\n        if para.get('id') in modified_para_ids:\n            # Find and remove all hp:lineSegArray children\n            for child in list(para):\n                if child.tag == f'{HP_TAG}lineSegArray':\n                    para.remove(child)\n                elif child.tag == f'{HP_TAG}lineSeg':\n                    para.remove(child)\n\n    # Convert to bytes with proper XML declaration\n    return ET.tostring(root, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    # Load JSON data\n    data = load_json_data(JSON_PATH)\n    print(f\"Loaded data: {data}\")\n\n    # Verify budget normalization\n    print(f\"Budget normalized: {normalize_budget(data['예산'])}\")\n\n    # Process XML files\n    section0_path = WORK_DIR / 'Contents' / 'section0.xml'\n    section1_path = WORK_DIR / 'Contents' / 'section1.xml'\n\n    section0_xml = process_xml_file(section0_path, data)\n    section1_xml = process_xml_file(section1_path, data)\n\n    # Verify no placeholders remain\n    section0_str = section0_xml.decode('utf-8')\n    section1_str = section1_xml.decode('utf-8')\n\n    if '{{' in section0_str or '{{' in section1_str:\n        print(\"ERROR: Placeholders still remain in the output!\")\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', section0_str):\n            print(f\"  Remaining in section0: {m.group()}\")\n        for m in re.finditer(r'\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}', section1_str):\n            print(f\"  Remaining in section1: {m.group()}\")\n        raise ValueError(\"Unreplaced placeholders found!\")\n\n    print(\"All placeholders replaced successfully.\")\n\n    # Namespace\n    ns = {'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'}\n\n    # Track modified paragraphs\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    # Process all elements\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        # Process text content\n        if elem.text:\n            new_text, text_modified = replace_placeholders_in_text(elem.text, data)\n            if text_modified:\n                elem.text = new_text\n                # Mark parent paragraph as modified\n                parent = elem\n                while parent is not None:\n                    if parent.tag == '{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}p':\n                        modified_paras.add(parent)\n                        break\n                    parent = parent.find('..')\n\n            #
